Adam algorithm:
- Parameter y = log(a) (initialized log(a + eps) maybe). But sum constraint handled by a=softmax(y)*n. If we update y and renormalize, equivalent. We can keep a and directly apply multiplicative updates with AdaGrad on gradients.
Option A: Keep a current normalized. Compute gradient g (derivative of score w.r.t a on sum-fixed manifold? The raw gradient; center it). Use update a_new = a * exp(-eta * m) / sum; m maybe Adam normalized gradient. This is exponentiated gradient.
Use momentum m = β1*m + (1-β1)*g; keep second moment v? g scale varies; can normalize g by its std or max. Use eta adapt.
Pseudo:
eta=0.2, beta1=0.8, g=grad_centered/scale; mom=beta1*mom+(1-beta1)*g; y=log(a)+eps; new_log = y + eta * (-mom + noise); a_new=exp(new_log - max)*n/sum.
But y accumulates; if we subtract mean to keep dynamic. Could just use log(a) each time (not preserve previous momentum in log domain?) We can maintain log_state? Simpler: a = exp(-eta*mom)/sum *n* best? Previous multiplicative was good. But full gradient maybe requires smaller eta because g magnitude large.

Maybe use Adam on θ where a_i ∝ exp(θ_i) with no normalization? After gradient, θ=θ - lr*grad_norm; normalize. Need keep θ vector. grad for objective on normalized a: Since a=exp(θ)/Z *n, objective depends on normalized a. Raw gradient d f/d a_i; under a_i = n e^{θ_i}/Z, projection? Could use direct normalized update: θ_i -= lr * (G_i - mean(G)) where G = raw gradient of f*scale? Then a_i new = exp(θ) normalized. This is gradient in log geometry. Good.

Let's implement generic `log_step(a, theta, G, lr, momentum, noise)`:
- Center G: G -= mean(G) (to preserve sum approximately; though normalization anyway, centering removes scale direction).
- Scale G: G /= (std(G)+eps) maybe for stable lr.
- Update momentum state m_i = β m +(1-β)G.
- theta_i -= lr * m_i (since minimize f; gradient G). Add noise σ N(0,1) to theta.
- a_new = softmax(theta)*n. But theta can have large offsets; subtract max.
- If a_new has too many tiny values? okay.
- Score; if improved update best; accept if score <= current*(1+tol)+... else reduce lr.
This is a variant of multiplicative with centroid. But if we maintain theta, normalization via softmax means small absolute theta changes have diminishing effect for large dynamic range; okay. Need initialize theta=log(a/mean) maybe.

Alternatively, keep a and use a_i *= exp(-lr*m); normalize; avoid theta overflow. Both similar. With theta, noise accumulates; can renormalize theta by subtract mean after each accepted step? Subtracted changes do not affect softmax. This can control magnitude.

We can combine with occasional restart/perturbation using low-frequency noise.

Now, what objective weights to use?
- Active max: v_k = 1/K for top K, else 0. G = gradient of max approximate. This directly lowers peaks.
- Softmax: v_k = softmax(β c). For β ~ 5? Need dynamic: c range maybe [0, n]; use (c-cmax)/scale. Compute weights stable: z=β*(c-cmax); weights=exp(z); sum; weights /= sum. For active K only top? Softmax across all coefficients will allocate tiny to all, causing gradient for low areas too. Full gradient can be strong where c high; good for flattening. β maybe 10-100. But if β too high, top-K-like.
- Flatten L2: v_k = (c_k - cmean)/var? This reduces variance. We can add small positive/negative weights. Negative weights may cause instability; but if centered, okay. Maybe use squared loss with weights v=2(c - c̄) and maybe regularize. However loss ∑(c-cbar)² can have negative weights: gradient step increases a for low c contributions? That should flatten. But since c≈S²/(2n) average, low coefficients near ends get negative weights, making them grow; can reduce max. Good. Need keep values nonnegative; softmax positivity.
- Combined weights: w = softmax_β(c) + λ * c_normalized + μ * topactive. Need ensure not too explosive. Could alternate.

Since objective is max, softmax high on max enough; flatten L2 may globally reduce variance. Try both.

We can also do "peak shaving" constraints: For each coefficient above threshold B, set weight p_k proportional (c_k - B)+; this focuses on exceeding target. Choose B = target*? Let's design a robust local search function:

`local_search(start, budget, seed)`:
Input start vector length n. It maintains `a_mu = best`, `a_cur`. It uses `y = log(a_cur + eps)`? Let's use direct multiplicative updates with momentum on `a` not theta, to avoid overflow and to allow zeros? If a has zeros, log impossible. Use a with floor: a_i = max(a, floor). But zeros maybe useful? Floor small prevents collapse. But optimum may have zeros; floor ~1e-8 okay.
Update: a_new = a * exp(logf); a_new = normalize(a_new). logf = -eta * grad_centered_scaled + noise. If a becomes all floor? fallback. Use `a_cur` current (may be above best). If a has zeros, use `np.log(a + 1e-12)` not needed; direct multiplicative.
If a_i zero and logf negative, stays zero; if positive, increases. Good.
Gradient scale: Use raw gradient g = grad_weights(w,a). Center g: g -= mean(g). Its max can be large. Normalize by max(abs(g)) or RMS. For top-K, g ~a contributions; scale ~1. Use step scale.
Use adaptive eta:
- start eta=0.1 (for direct multiplicative). If objective improves, eta *=1.05 up to max 1; if worsens bad, eta *=0.7.
- accept if new_score <= cur_score*(1+accept_rel), accept_rel start 0.05 decay to 0.005. Move current to new even if not best? Use simulated annealing: accept with probability? Simpler accept if not much worse.
- Use momentum m = β*m + (1-β)*g_scaled. Use β=0.5 initially, maybe 0.7.
- Noise: add small white/smooth noise to logf to escape. Noise amplitude adaptive.
- Periodically reseed from best with smooth perturbation.

Top-K gradient function:
```
def grad_topk(a,c,K):
 n=len(a); d=zeros(n)
 if K>=len(c): idx=arange(len(c))
 else: idx=argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
 inv=1.0/K
 for k in idx:
   s=max(0,k-n+1); e=min(n,k+1); a_seg = a[k-e+1:k-s+1][::-1]? Let's verify.
   For coefficient k, valid i from max(0,k-(n-1)) to min(n-1,k). h=k-i. ranges: when i=s..e-1, h=k-i. h decreases as i increases. We need add a[h]. We can slice a[k-(e-1) : k-s+1] reversed. Example k=5,n=5: s=1,e=5, i=1..4, h=4,3,2,1. a[1:5] reversed? k-e+1=1, k-s+1=5, a[1:5] reversed gives h=4,3,2,1 yes. Good.
   d[s:e] += inv*a_slice[::-1]
 return 2*d
```
We can sort idx? Not needed.
Potential repeated updates from k and 2(n-1)-k maybe? Fine.

For full softmax gradient via convolution:
```
def grad_weights(w,a):
  n=len(a); L=2n-1
  ar = a[::-1]
  N=1 << (L+n-2).bit_length()  # conv len L+n-1? Wait len(w)=L, len(ar)=n; conv length L+n-1=3n-2. Need N≥3n-2.
  W=fft(w,N); A=fft(ar,N); conv=ifft(W*A, N).real[:L+n-1]
  g = 2*conv[n-1:n-1+n]
  return g
```

`normalize_sequence(a,target)`: Clip finite, nonneg, clip max=1000. If sum zero fill target/n. Then scale to target. If max after scaling >1000, scale down? For target n, max likely ≤1000. But if a has spikes, scale could exceed 1000; clip loops. Function:
```
def normalize_sequence(a,target):
 a=np.asarray(...).copy()
 a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0)
 a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
 n=a.size
 for _ in range(3):
  s=a.sum()
  if s<=1e-15: a.fill(target/n)[:...]; break
  a *= target/s
  a = np.clip(a,0,1000)
 s=a.sum()
 if s<=...: a.fill(target/n)
 else:
   # if max 1000 prevent further scaling? If target/s would exceed 1000 max, clip and maybe sum < target.
   new=a*(target/s)
   if new.max()<=1000: a=new
   else: a=np.clip(new,0,1000); # sum maybe target not, okay? Objective can handle.
 return a
```
For search, we want exact sum target. If clipping needed, we can set target smaller? But values should not exceed 1000. For n up to 16384 target 16384, equal 1. Spikes maybe <1000. Fine.

Local search details in code:
```
def local_search(start_a, budget_s, seed=0, verbose=False):
    n=int(len(start_a)); a=normalize_sequence(start_a,float(n))
    rng=default_rng(seed)
    t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+max(1,budget_s)
    c=fast_convolution(a); score=fast_score_from(a,c,n)
    best=a.copy(); best_score=score; cur=a.copy(); cur_score=score; c_cur=c
    mom=zeros(n); it=0; eta=0.12; beta=0.55; noise=0.02; accept=0.06; stag=0; best_eta=eta
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       it+=1
       # If time to break
       if stag > 1200: reset perturbation; if still no improve after 3000 break?
       # choose weights/gradient
       mode = it % 24
       if mode <18: topK
       elif mode <22: full softmax with tau=5+20*(it//100)
       else: flatten (c-cmean) * scale
       compute g
       g center, scale
       if scale small -> maybe reset
       update mom
       logf = -eta * mom + rng.normal(0,noise,n)
       logf = clip(logf,-1.5,1.5)
       cand = a * exp(logf); normalize
       c2=fast_convolution(cand); score2=fast_score
       if finite:
          if score2 < best_score: best=... stag=0
          if score2 <= cur_score*(1+accept) + 1e-12:
              cur=cand; cur_score=score2; c=c2; mom*=0.8
              if score2 < cur? eta=min(1.2,eta*1.04) else eta=max(0.02,eta*0.98)
              accept=max(0.001, accept*0.999)
              noise=max(0.002, noise*0.999)
              stag += 1
          else:
              eta=max(0.02, eta*0.75); mom*=0.5; stag+=1
       else: eta*=0.7; ...
       if stag>500: reset from best with perturbation; eta=0.2; mom=0; accept=0.08; noise=0.05; stag=0
       if best_score <= lower? maybe 1.0? but impossible.
    return best, best_score
```

```
def smooth_perturb(a,rng,strength=0.25):
  n=len(a)
```

Let's write pseudo now and inspect for bugs.

```
def next_pow2(m):
    if m<=1: return 1
    return 1 << (int(m)-1).bit_length()
```

`fast_convolution`:
```
def fast_convolution(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.empty(0)
    L=2*n-1
    N=next_pow2(L)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)[:L]
    np.maximum(c,0,out=c)
    return c
```

`fast_score_array`:
```
def fast_score_array(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    if a.size==0: return inf
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0)
    a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<0.01: return inf
    c=fast_convolution(a)
    if c.size==0: return inf
    m=c.max()
    return float(2.0*a.size*m/(s*s))
```

`fast_score_from_c`:
```
def fast_score_from(a,c):
    s=a.sum(); n=a.size; m=max(0,float(c.max())); if s<0.01: return inf; return 2.0*n*m/(s*s)
```

`normalize_sequence`:
```
def normalize_sequence(a,target):
    a=np.array(a,float,copy=True)
    if a.size==0: return a
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0)
    a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
    n=a.size
    for _ in range(4):
       s=float(a.sum())
       if s<=1e-15:
          a.fill(target/float(n)); break
       new=a*(target/s)
       if new.max()<=1000.0:
          a=new
       else:
          a=np.clip(new,0,1000)
       s2=float(a.sum())
       if abs(s2-target) <= 1e-6*max(1,target): break
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s<=1e-15:
       a.fill(target/float(n))
    else:
       # final scale if possible
       new=a*(target/s)
       if new.max()<=1000: a=new
       else: a=np.clip(new,0,1000)
    return a
```
This may not exactly sum if clipping. Good.

`upsample_sequence`:
```
def upsample_sequence(seq,n):
   seq=np.asarray(seq,float)
   m=seq.size; n=int(n)
   if m==0: return normalize_sequence(np.ones(n),n)
   if m==1: return np.full(n,float(seq[0]) if sum? target?)
   if m==n: return normalize_sequence(seq,n)
   xold=np.linspace(0,1,m); xnew=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   out=np.interp(xnew,xold,seq)
   return normalize_sequence(out,n)
```

```
def add_start(pool, a, n, required_min_score=0):

      sc=fast_score_array(a)
      if np.isfinite(sc): pool.append((float(sc), a))
   except: pass
```

`make_anti_smooth(n,rng,alpha,gamma, harmonic_count)`:
Generate p anti by summing odd sin/cos? To ensure anti: define base u on first half random, reflect negative.
Simpler function:
```
def make_anti_p(n,rng,max_harm=5):
   m=n//2
   p=np.zeros(n)
   if m==0: return p
   # For n even or odd, p indices i and n-1-i anti.
   # Generate p for first half from harmonics with t in [0,1] for i=0..m-1
   t=np.linspace(0,1,m+1)[:-1] if m>0 else []
   y=np.zeros(m)
   for h in range(1,max_harm+1):
      amp=rng.uniform(-1,1)/h
      ph=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
      y += amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*h*t + ph)  # arbitrary first half
   y -= np.mean(y) # optional
   maxabs=np.max(np.abs(y))
   if maxabs>1e-12: y/=maxabs
   # assign
   p[:m]=y
   if n%2==0:
      p[m:] = -y[::-1]
   else:
      p[m+1:] = -y[::-1]
      p[m]=0.0
   # scale to max1
   ma=np.max(np.abs(p))
   if ma>0: p/=ma
   return p
```
This p anti-discrete. Then x=1+alpha*p, alpha∈[0.2,1]. Note p may not be smooth across boundary if y endpoint; local search smooths.
Could also use Gaussian anti: y = exp(-(i/m -0.5)^2/σ²) -0.5 etc. We'll random harmonic.

```
return normalize_sequence(a,n)
```

Local search code with full gradient:

```
def grad_topk(a,c,K):
 n=a.size; L=c.size; d=np.zeros(n)
 if K>=L: idx=np.arange(L)
 else: idx=np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
 inv=1.0/float(K)
 n1=n-1
 for k in idx:
   k=int(k)
   s=max(0,k-n1); e=min(n,k+1)
   if e>s:
     # a segment h from k-e+1 to k-s
     seg_start=k-(e-1)
     seg_end=k-s+1
     d[s:e] += inv * a[seg_start:seg_end][::-1]
 return 2.0*d
```
Check seg bounds: For n=5,k=0: s=0,e=1, seg_start=0, seg_end=1; d[0]+=a[0]. Good.
For k=2n-2: s=n-1,e=n, seg_start=k-n+1? e-1=n-1 => k-n+1 = n-1; seg_end=k-s+1=2n-2-(n-1)+1=n; a[n-1:n] reversed; good.

```
def grad_full_weights(a,w):
 n=a.size; L=w.size; # L=2n-1
 N=next_pow2(3*n-2)
 WR=np.asarray(w[::-1],float)
 fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
 fw=np.fft.rfft(WR,N)
 conv=np.fft.irfft(fa*fw,N)
 seg=conv[n-1:2*n-1]
 return 2.0*seg[::-1].copy()
```
If w contains NaN? Ensure finite. `w = np.where(np.isfinite(w),w,0)`.

```
def centered_scaled(g):
 g=np.asarray(g,float)
 g-=g.mean()
 norm=np.sqrt(np.mean(g*g))
 if norm<1e-14: return None
 return g/norm
```

```
def run_level(n,budget,prev,seed,verbose):
 n=int(n)
 budget=max(1.0,budget)
 t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+budget
 if budget<10 or n<16:
   base=upsample(prev,n) if prev is not None else np.ones(n); return base, fast_score_array(base)
 rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
 starts=[]
 count_random = max(8, min(40, int(budget//5)+5))? For 600s, 120 cap 40.
 generate starts with `generate_starts(n,rng,prev,count)`.
 starts.sort
 # Determine number to refine: at least 1, min 5. If budget small, maybe 2. k=min(len(starts), max(1, int(budget/60)+1), 5)
 per=(deadline-now)/k
 best_seq=None; best_score=inf
 for i in range(k):
   if time>deadline-2 break
   sc,seq=starts[i]
   if sc<best_score: best_seq=seq.copy(); best_score=sc
   time_this=max(5, min(per, deadline-now-2))
   try: seq2,sc2=local_search(seq,time_this,seed=seed*10+i,...)
   except: seq2=sc,seq
   if sc2<best_score: update
   # Maybe pass improved to next start? If best improved, add start upsample best? not needed.
 if best_seq is None: fallback ones
 return best_seq,best_score
```

`make_a` function top-level:
```
def softmax_a(theta,n):
  t=theta - np.max(theta)
  e=np.exp(t)
  s=e.sum()
  if s<=1e-300: e=np.ones_like(theta)
  return (float(n)/float(e.sum()))*e
```

Potential local search with theta: If we apply theta -= gradient, is gradient sign correct? Objective increases with a components that increase peaks. We want decrease them. The direction in θ for descent is -g_i (plus normalization constant). So `theta -= eta*g`. We used step=-eta*mom, so yes.

At optimum for active maximum set A and positive a, KKT: For all i in support, λ * ∑_{k∈A} μ_k a_{k-i} = constant, where ∑ μ_k=1, c_k≤M for active =M. This is a nonlinear eigenvalue-like. We can approximate by iterating:
- Given a, find active set A and weights μ that make A(a)** μ as constant as possible, where (A(a)**)_i =∑ μ_k a_{k-i}. We want μ to minimize variance of this vector (or maximize min). μ can be solved by least squares? This is dual certificate. Then update a? Not direct.
- Given μ, the active constraint objective linear in c? If we take max over A with weights μ, f=∑ μ_k c_k. For fixed μ, minimize f subject a≥0, sum n, and maybe c_k≤M. This is a quadratic form a^T B a where B depends on μ, not PSD. But we can minimize f by subgradient ∂f/∂a =2 a^T? This is our gradient with μ weights.
Thus our top-weight method is dual ascent. Could optimize μ adaptively as softmax of peaks (which we do). Good.

Let's code:
```
def softmax_weights(c, tau):
    cmax=float(c.max())
    scale=float(np.std(c))
    if scale<1e-12:
        w=np.zeros_like(c); w[np.argmax(c)]=1.0; return w
    z=(c-cmax)/scale
    # clip z to [-50,0] to avoid underflow? z max 0, min maybe -inf.
    np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0, out=z)
    w=np.exp(tau*z)
    s=w.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: w zeros top
    else: w/=s
    return w
```
If tau large, many underflow but max remains.

Local search implementation details:
```
def local_search(start, budget_s, seed=0, verbose=False):
    a = np.asarray(start,float); n=a.size
    target=float(n)
    a=normalize_sequence(a,target)
    t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+max(0.5,float(budget_s))
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    c=fast_convolution(a)
    score=fast_score_from(a,c)
    best=a.copy(); best_score=score
    theta=np.log(a+1e-15); theta-=theta.mean()
    mom=np.zeros(n)
    eta=0.3? Let's set 0.35? Need stable. With theta, step in log; eta 0.3 maybe too high? For normalized gradient RMS 1, eta 0.3 changes values by exp(0.3)~35%; maybe okay with acceptance. Previous eta 0.25. Start 0.25, max 0.8.
    beta_m=0.55
    accept=0.08
    noise=0.03
    K_base=max(8, min(128, (2*n)//256))
    stag=0; reset_cnt=0; best_reset_score=best_score
    while ...:
       if stag > 2000: break maybe? Or reset.
       it+=1
       # if time near deadline break
       # mode
       if it % 30 == 0:
          w=flatten_weights(c); g=grad_full_weights(a,w); eta_use=0.6*eta
       elif it % 7 == 0:
          tau=10.0 + min(80.0, 0.01*it)
          w=softmax_weights(c,tau); g=grad_full_weights(a,w); eta_use=0.8*eta
       else:
          K=K_base
          if stag>150: K=min(256,K*2)
          if stag>400: K=min(256,K*3)
          g=grad_topk(a,c,K)
          eta_use=eta
       gsc=centered_scaled(g)
       if gsc is None: ...
       mom = beta_m*mom + (1-beta_m)*gsc
       mom_sc=centered_scaled(mom)
       if mom_sc is None: stag+=1; continue
       step = -eta_use * mom_sc
       if noise>1e-4: step += rng.normal(0,noise,n)
       step = np.clip(step,-1.5,1.5)
       theta_cand = theta + step
       theta_cand -= theta_cand.mean()
       cand = softmax_a(theta_cand,n)
       c2=fast_convolution(cand)
       sc2=fast_score_from(cand,c2)
       if not finite: eta=max(0.02,eta*0.75); stag+=1; continue
       if sc2 < best_score:
          best=cand.copy(); best_score=sc2; stag=0
       if sc2 <= score*(1+accept)+1e-12:
          a=cand; c=c2; score=sc2; theta=theta_cand; mom*=0.85
          if sc2 < score_prev? but score updated. Need old_score before update.
          old=score
          if sc2 < old*(1-1e-10):
             eta=min(0.8,eta*1.05)
          else:
             eta=max(0.02,eta*0.98)
          accept=max(0.001, accept*0.9995)
          noise=max(0.003, noise*0.9995)
          stag += 1
       else:
          eta=max(0.02,eta*0.75); mom*=0.5; stag+=1
       if stag>300:
          # reset
          reset_cnt+=1
          strength=0.2 + 0.08*min(6, reset_cnt)
          a=smooth_perturbation(best, rng, strength)
          c=fast_convolution(a); score=fast_score_from(a,c)
          theta=np.log(a+1e-15); theta-=theta.mean()
          mom=np.zeros(n); eta=0.35; accept=0.1; noise=0.06; stag=0
          if reset_cnt>12 and score>best_score*1.1: # maybe generate new anti random
              a=make_anti_sequence(n,rng, rng.uniform(0.5,1.0))
              c=...; score=...
              theta=...
              if score < best_score? update? but best remains.
              reset_cnt=0
       if verbose and it%500==0: print...
    return best,best_score
```

```
def make_anti_sequence(n,rng,alpha=None):

  if alpha is None: alpha=rng.uniform(0.4,1.0)
  a=1.0 + alpha*p
```

`make_tanh_p` normalized:
```
def make_tanh_p(n,gamma):
 if n<=1: return zeros
 t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
 p=np.tanh(gamma*(2*t-1))
 ma=np.max(np.abs(p))
 if ma>0: p/=ma
 return p
```
Since t=0,1, p even? p[0]=tanh(-gamma), p[-1]=tanh(gamma); anti p[::-1]=-p if n? t[-1]=1; 2*1-1=1; yes.
For n even, pair t_i and 1-t_i exact? linspace includes symmetric. Good.

`make_binomial_p` for n large recurrence O(n). Normalize by max. If all zeros (n=1) return zeros.

`make_slant` and `make_power`:
```
def make_slant(n,beta):
 t=np.linspace(0,1,n) if n>1 else [0]
 a=1+beta*(t-0.5)
 return normalize_sequence(np.clip(a,0,1000),float(n))
def make_power(n,p,rev=False):
 t=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/float(n) if n else [0]
 a=np.power(t+1e-6,p)
 if rev: a=a[::-1]
 return normalize_sequence(a,float(n))
```
For p negative near zero values large; normalize maybe spikes; okay.

```
def get_previous_sequence():
  try:
    seq=globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
    if seq is None: return None
    arr=np.asarray(seq,float)
    if arr.size==0: return None
    arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr),arr,0)
    arr=np.clip(arr,0,1000)
    return arr
  except: return None
```

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(a,a))/(sum(a))^2.

The core non-convex optimization is a projected multiplicative/softmax
subgradient search in log-space.  It alternates between:
  * focused top-peak subgradients,
  * flat softmax surrogates over all convolution coefficients,
  * variance-flattening L2 gradient accents.

The search is multi-resolution: promising sequences are polished and
upsampled to longer lengths.  Several structurally-motivated non-negative
starts are used, including anti-palindromic tanh/binomial shapes, smooth
random shapes, slants, and powers.  Finally the best candidates are verified
with the exact evaluator before returning.
"""

import numpy as np
import time


def _pair_score(pair):
    return pair[0]


def next_power_of_two(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_sequence(a, target):
    """
    Clip to valid range and rescale to sum target.  If clipping would violate
    the max value cap, the sum may be slightly smaller.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
    if a.size == 0:
        return a

    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)

    n = float(a.size)
    target = float(target)

    for _ in range(4):
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s <= 1e-15:
            a.fill(target / max(1.0, n))
            break

        new = a * (target / s)
        if float(new.max()) <= 1000.0:
            a = new
        else:
            a = np.clip(new, 0.0, 1000.0)

        s2 = float(a.sum())
        if abs(s2 - target) <= 1e-7 * max(1.0, target):
            break

    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        a.fill(target / max(1.0, n))
    else:
        new = a * (target / s)
        if float(new.max()) <= 1000.0:
            a = new
        else:
            a = np.clip(new, 0.0, 1000.0)

    return a


def fast_convolution(a):
    """FFT-based linear convolution a*a, length 2n-1."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = next_power_of_two(L)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)[:L]
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c


def fast_score_array(a):
    """Fast analogue of evaluate_sequence(), without the direct O(n^2) convolution."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")

    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)

    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        return float("inf")

    c = fast_convolution(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")

    m = float(c.max())
    return float(2.0 * a.size * m / (s * s))


def fast_score_from(a, c):
    """Score from an already computed convolution."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        return float("inf")
    m = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
    return float(2.0 * a.size * m / (s * s))


def softmax_a(theta, n):
    """Softmax array scaled to sum n."""
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64)
    n = float(n)
    if theta.size == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    t = theta - float(np.max(theta))
    e = np.exp(t)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        e = np.ones_like(theta)
        s = float(e.size)
    return (n / s) * e


def upsample_sequence(seq, n):
    """Linearly interpolate to length n and renormalize."""
    seq = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(n)
    m = int(seq.size)

    if n <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if m == 0:
        return normalize_sequence(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), float(n))
    if m == n:
        return normalize_sequence(seq, float(n))

    xold = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    xnew = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    out = np.interp(xnew, xold, seq)
    return normalize_sequence(out, float(n))


def add_to_start_pool(pool, a, n):
    """Normalize, fast-evaluate, and append a start if finite."""
    try:
        n = int(n)
        a = normalize_sequence(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64), float(n))
        sc = fast_score_array(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            pool.append((float(sc), a))
    except Exception:
        pass


def make_slant(n, beta):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1:
        return normalize_sequence(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), float(n))
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    a = 1.0 + float(beta) * (t - 0.5)
    return normalize_sequence(np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0), float(n))


def make_power(n, p, reverse=False):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1:
        return normalize_sequence(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), float(n))
    t = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    a = np.power(t + 1e-9, float(p))
    if reverse:
        a = a[::-1]
    return normalize_sequence(a, float(n))


def make_binomial_anti_p(n):
    """
    Anti-palindromic shape p[-1-i] = -p[i] derived from coefficients of
    (1-z)^(-1/2):  p_i ~ (b_i - b_{n-1-i})/(b_i + b_{n-1-i}).
    """
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    b = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b[0] = 1.0
    for k in range(1, n):
        b[k] = b[k - 1] * (2.0 * k - 1.0) / (2.0 * k)

    rev = b[::-1]
    p = (b - rev) / (b + rev + 1e-300)
    ma = float(np.max(np.abs(p)))
    if ma > 1e-300:
        p /= ma
    return p


def make_tanh_anti_p(n, gamma):
    """Normalized anti-palindromic tanh( gamma*(2t-1) )."""
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    p = np.tanh(float(gamma) * (2.0 * t - 1.0))
    ma = float(np.max(np.abs(p)))
    if ma > 1e-300:
        p /= ma
    return p


def make_anti_p_random(n, rng, harm=5):
    """Random smooth anti-palindromic p with p_{n-1-i} = -p_i."""
    n = int(n)
    p = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 2:
        return p

    m = n // 2
    if m <= 0:
        return p

    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    y = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)

    for h in range(1, int(harm) + 1):
        amp = rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0) / float(h + 0.25)
        ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        y += amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * h * t + ph)

    y -= float(np.mean(y))
    ma = float(np.max(np.abs(y)))
    if ma > 1e-300:
        y /= ma

    p[:m] = y
    if n % 2 == 0:
        p[m:] = -y[::-1]
    else:
        p[m:] = 0.0
        p[m + 1:] = -y[::-1]

    ma = float(np.max(np.abs(p)))
    if ma > 1e-300:
        p /= ma
    return p


def make_anti_sequence(n, rng, alpha=None, sign_binary_prob=0.25):
    """Non-negative normalized sequence 1 + alpha * p, where p is anti-palindromic."""
    n = int(n)
    p = make_anti_p_random(n, rng)
    if alpha is None:
        alpha = float(rng.uniform(0.40, 1.00))

    if np.random.default_rng is None:
        pass

    if float(rng.random()) < sign_binary_prob:
        p = np.sign(p)

    a = 1.0 + alpha * p
    return normalize_sequence(np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0), float(n))


def make_random_smooth(n, rng, strength=1.0):
    """Smooth positive random sequence."""
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1:
        return normalize_sequence(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), float(n))

    z = rng.normal(size=n)
    w = max(3, int(n // 2000))
    ker = np.ones(w, dtype=np.float64) / float(w)

    z = np.convolve(z, ker, mode="same")
    z = np.convolve(z, ker, mode="same")

    zm = float(np.mean(z))
    zs = float(np.std(z))
    if zs > 1e-12:
        z = (z - zm) / zs
    else:
        z = z - zm

    a = np.exp(float(strength) * z)
    return normalize_sequence(a, float(n))


def smooth_perturbation(a, rng, strength=0.30):
    """Multiplicative smooth perturbation from a base sequence."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 1:
        return normalize_sequence(a, float(n))

    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    z = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    for h in range(1, 6):
        amp = float(strength) / (0.5 + 0.5 * h)
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        z += amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * h * t + ph)

    a *= np.exp(z)

    p = make_anti_p_random(n, rng, harm=4)
    a *= np.exp(0.35 * float(strength) * p)

    a += rng.normal(0.0, 0.01 * float(strength), size=n)
    a = np.clip(a, 1e-12, 1000.0)
    return normalize_sequence(a, float(n))


def generate_starts(n, rng, previous, random_count=24):
    """Generate a diverse pool of starts for the given length."""
    n = int(n)
    pool = []
    random_count = int(max(6, min(36, random_count)))

    if previous is not None and previous.size > 0:
        up = upsample_sequence(previous, n)
        add_to_start_pool(pool, up, n)
        for s in (0.10, 0.25, 0.50, 0.85):
            add_to_start_pool(pool, smooth_perturbation(up, rng, s), n)

    # Simple deterministic families.
    add_to_start_pool(pool, np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), n)
    for beta in (-1.8, -1.2, -0.6, 0.0, 0.6, 1.2, 1.8):
        add_to_start_pool(pool, make_slant(n, beta), n)

    for p in (-1.5, -1.0, -0.5, 0.5, 1.0, 1.5):
        add_to_start_pool(pool, make_power(n, p, False), n)
        add_to_start_pool(pool, make_power(n, p, True), n)

    # Structured anti-palindromic starts.
    pbin = make_binomial_anti_p(n)
    if float(np.max(np.abs(pbin))) > 1e-12:
        for alpha in (0.40, 0.70, 1.00):
            add_to_start_pool(pool, 1.0 + alpha * pbin, n)

    if n > 8:
        for gamma in (1.0, 2.0, 5.0, 12.0):
            pt = make_tanh_anti_p(n, gamma)
            if float(np.max(np.abs(pt))) > 1e-12:
                for alpha in (0.50, 0.80, 1.00):
                    add_to_start_pool(pool, 1.0 + alpha * pt, n)

    # Random anti and random smooth starts.
    for _ in range(random_count):
        add_to_start_pool(pool, make_anti_sequence(n, rng), n)
    for _ in range(max(4, random_count // 3)):
        add_to_start_pool(pool, make_random_smooth(n, rng, rng.uniform(0.8, 1.6)), n)

    if not pool:
        pool.append((2.0, normalize_sequence(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), float(n))))

    pool.sort(key=_pair_score)
    return pool


def grad_topk(a, c, K):
    """
    Subgradient of the average of the top K convolution coefficients.
    Returns raw gradient with respect to a (sum-constraint direction not removed).
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    L = int(c.size)

    if n == 0 or L == 0:
        return np.zeros(max(n, 0), dtype=np.float64)

    K = int(max(1, min(K, L)))
    d = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    if K >= L:
        idx = np.arange(L)
    else:
        idx = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]

    inv = 1.0 / float(K)
    n1 = n - 1

    for kk in idx:
        kk = int(kk)
        s = max(0, kk - n1)
        e = min(n, kk + 1)
        if e > s:
            seg_start = kk - (e - 1)
            seg_end = kk - s + 1
            d[s:e] += inv * a[seg_start:seg_end][::-1]

    return 2.0 * d


def grad_full_weights(a, w):
    """
    Gradient of sum_k w_k c_k for c = a*a.
    Uses convolution with reversed weights:
        g_i = 2 * sum_j a_j w_{i+j}.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    L = int(w.size)

    if n == 0 or L == 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
    if float(np.sum(w)) == 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    need = 3 * n - 2
    N = next_power_of_two(need)

    wr = w[::-1].astype(np.float64, copy=False)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(wr, N)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(fa * fw, N)

    seg = conv[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    return (2.0 * seg[::-1]).copy()


def softmax_weights(c, tau):
    """Positive normalized weights emphasizing large convolution coefficients."""
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    if c.size == 0:
        return c

    w = np.zeros_like(c)
    cmax = float(c.max())
    if cmax <= 0.0:
        w[0] = 1.0
        return w

    scale = float(np.std(c))
    if scale < 1e-12:
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return w

    z = (c - cmax) / scale
    np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0, out=z)
    w = np.exp(float(tau) * z)

    s = float(w.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        w.fill(0.0)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        w /= s
    return w


def flatten_weights(c):
    """Weights proportional to c - mean(c), used for variance flattening."""
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    if c.size == 0:
        return c

    w = c - float(np.mean(c))
    s = float(np.std(w))
    if s > 1e-12:
        w /= s
    np.clip(w, -5.0, 5.0, out=w)
    return w


def centered_scaled(g):
    """Remove the scaling direction and normalize gradient gradient RMS."""
    g = np.asarray(g, dtype=np.float64)
    if g.size == 0:
        return None

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
        return None

    g = g - float(np.mean(g))
    norm = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(g * g)))
    if norm < 1e-14:
        return None
    return g / norm


def local_search(start_a, budget_s, seed=0, verbose=False):
    """
    Softmax-projection multiplicative subgradient/ascent-descent local search.
    Returns (best_sequence, best_fast_score).
    """
    start_a = np.asarray(start_a, dtype=np.float64)
    if start_a.size == 0:
        return start_a, float("inf")

    n = int(start_a.size)
    target = float(n)
    a = normalize_sequence(start_a, target)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(0.5, float(budget_s))

    if deadline - t0 < 3.0:
        c = fast_convolution(a)
        return a, fast_score_from(a, c)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    c = fast_convolution(a)
    score = fast_score_from(a, c)

    best = a.copy()
    best_score = float(score)

    theta = np.log(a + 1e-15)
    theta -= float(np.mean(theta))

    mom = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    eta = 0.30
    beta_mom = 0.55
    accept_rel = 0.08
    noise = 0.030
    K_base = int(max(8, min(128, (2 * n) // 256)))

    stag = 0
    resets = 0
    it = 0
    last_print = t0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1

        if stag > 5000:
            break

        # Choose mode.  Full transformations are used periodically; top-K is cheap.
        if it % 30 == 0:
            w = flatten_weights(c)
            g = grad_full_weights(a, w)
            eta_use = 0.55 * eta
        elif it % 7 == 0:
            tau = 10.0 + min(85.0, 0.008 * float(it))
            w = softmax_weights(c, tau)
            g = grad_full_weights(a, w)
            eta_use = 0.75 * eta
        else:
            K = K_base
            if stag > 150:
                K = min(256, 2 * K)
            if stag > 450:
                K = min(256, 3 * K)
            g = grad_topk(a, c, K)
            eta_use = eta

        gsc = centered_scaled(g)
        if gsc is None:
            stag += 1
            continue

        mom = beta_mom * mom + (1.0 - beta_mom) * gsc
        msc = centered_scaled(mom)
        if msc is None:
            stag += 1
            continue

        step = -eta_use * msc
        if noise > 1e-4:
            step = step + rng.normal(0.0, noise, n)

        step = np.clip(step, -1.6, 1.6)
        theta_cand = theta + step
        theta_cand -= float(np.mean(theta_cand))

        cand = softmax_a(theta_cand, n)
        c2 = fast_convolution(cand)
        sc2 = fast_score_from(cand, c2)

        if not np.isfinite(sc2):
            eta = max(0.02, eta * 0.75)
            mom *= 0.5
            stag += 1
            continue

        if sc2 < best_score:
            best = cand.copy()
            best_score = sc2
            stag = 0

        if sc2 <= score * (1.0 + accept_rel) + 1e-12:
            old = score
            a = cand
            c = c2
            score = sc2
            theta = theta_cand
            mom *= 0.85

            if sc2 < old * (1.0 - 1e-11):
                eta = min(0.95, eta * 1.055)
            else:
                eta = max(0.02, eta * 0.985)

            accept_rel = max(0.0015, accept_rel * 0.9997)
            noise = max(0.0025, noise * 0.9997)
            stag += 1
        else:
            eta = max(0.02, eta * 0.75)
            mom *= 0.5
            stag += 1

        if stag > 320:
            resets += 1
            strength = 0.22 + 0.07 * min(8, resets)
            a_new = smooth_perturbation(best, rng, strength)
            c_new = fast_convolution(a_new)
            sc_new = fast_score_from(a_new, c_new)

            if not np.isfinite(sc_new):
                a_new = best.copy()
                c_new = fast_convolution(best)
                sc_new = best_score

            a = a_new
            c = c_new
            score = sc_new
            theta = np.log(a + 1e-15)
            theta -= float(np.mean(theta))
            mom = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            eta = 0.35
            accept_rel = 0.10
            noise = 0.055
            stag = 0

            if resets > 22:
                # Occasional fresh random start, but never discard best.
                a_new = make_anti_sequence(n, rng, alpha=rng.uniform(0.5, 1.0))
                c_new = fast_convolution(a_new)
                sc_new = fast_score_from(a_new, c_new)
                if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < best_score:
                    best = a_new.copy()
                    best_score = sc_new
                a = a_new
                c = c_new
                score = sc_new
                theta = np.log(a + 1e-15)
                theta -= float(np.mean(theta))
                mom = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                resets = 0
                stag = 0

            if resets > 35:
                break

        if verbose:
            nw = time.monotonic()
            if nw - last_print > 10.0:
                last_print = nw
                print(f"    local_search n={n} it={it} best={best_score:.8f} cur={score:.8f}")

    return best, float(best_score)


def run_level(n, budget_s, previous_best, seed=0):
    """Generate starts for length n and locally refine the best ones."""
    n = int(n)
    budget_s = max(0.0, float(budget_s))

    if previous_best is None or previous_best.size == 0:
        base = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        base = upsample_sequence(previous_best, n)

    if budget_s < 6.0:
        sc = fast_score_array(base)
        return base, float(sc)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + budget_s

    random_count = int(max(8, min(34, budget_s / 18.0 + 4.0)))
    starts = generate_starts(n, rng, previous_best, random_count=random_count)

    now = time.monotonic()
    remain = max(0.0, deadline - now)
    if remain <= 2.0:
        return starts[0][1], starts[0][0]

    k = min(len(starts), max(1, int(remain / 120.0) + 1), 5)
    time_each = remain / float(k)

    best_seq = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    for i in range(k):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= deadline - 2.0:
            break

        sc, seq = starts[i]
        if sc < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc)
            best_seq = seq.copy()

        local_budget = min(time_each, deadline - now - 1.5)
        if local_budget < 4.0:
            continue

        try:
            seq2, sc2 = local_search(seq, local_budget, seed=int(seed * 101 + i + 17), verbose=False)
            if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score:
                best_score = float(sc2)
                best_seq = seq2.copy()
        except Exception:
            pass

    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = starts[0][1]
        best_score = starts[0][0]

    return best_seq, float(best_score)


def add_candidate_pool(pool, seq, score):
    """Maintain a sorted pool of candidates keyed by fast score."""
    if seq is None or score is None:
        return
    try:
        sc = float(score)
    except Exception:
        return
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return

    arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return

    pool.append((sc, arr))
    pool.sort(key=_pair_score)
    if len(pool) > 16:
        pool.pop()


def sanitize_for_return(seq):
    """Return a valid Python list of non-negative finite floats."""
    seq = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64)
    if seq.size == 0:
        return [1.0] * 512

    seq = np.where(np.isfinite(seq), seq, 0.0)
    seq = np.clip(seq, 0.0, 1000.0)

    s = float(seq.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        return [1.0] * 1024

    # Rescale to sum = len if possible.
    new = seq * (float(seq.size) / s)
    if float(new.max()) <= 1000.0:
        seq = new
    else:
        seq = np.clip(new, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s2 = float(seq.sum())
        if s2 < 0.01:
            return [1.0] * 1024

    return [float(x) for x in seq]


def exact_score_list(lst):
    """Call the supplied exact evaluator safely."""
    try:
        val = evaluate_sequence(lst)
        val = float(val)
        return val if np.isfinite(val) else float("inf")
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def get_previous_sequence():
    """Access height_sequence_1 global if present."""
    try:
        seq = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if seq is None:
            return None
        arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64)
        if arr.size == 0:
            return None
        arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        return arr
    except Exception:
        return None


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Main search entry point.

    Runs a coarse-to-fine multi-resolution search.  Long lengths get most of the
    time, after which the best candidate is locally polished once more.  A few top
    candidates are verified with the exact evaluator before returning the best one.
    """
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(120.0, float(budget_s))

    # Reserve enough time for exact validation and final cleanup.
    if budget > 1200.0:
        reserve = 240.0
    elif budget > 600.0:
        reserve = 160.0
    else:
        reserve = 90.0

    search_deadline = t0 + max(60.0, budget - reserve)

    prev = get_previous_sequence()

    # Initial global best.
    global_seq = None
    global_score = float("inf")

    if prev is not None and prev.size > 0:
        # Exact baseline for the supplied construction, if not too expensive.
        if prev.size <= 20000:
            base_exact = exact_score_list([float(x) for x in prev])
            if np.isfinite(base_exact):
                global_seq = normalize_sequence(prev, float(prev.size))
                global_score = base_exact

    if global_seq is None:
        n0 = 512
        global_seq = normalize_sequence(np.ones(n0, dtype=np.float64), float(n0))
        global_score = fast_score_array(global_seq)

    # Candidate pool.
    candidate_pool = []
    add_candidate_pool(candidate_pool, global_seq, global_score)

    # Determine level plan.
    if budget > 1800.0:
        max_n = 16384
    elif budget > 1100.0:
        max_n = 8192
    else:
        max_n = 4096

    base_levels = [512, 1024, 2048, 4096, 8192, 16384]
    levels = set()

    if prev is not None and prev.size > 0 and prev.size <= max_n and prev.size >= 128:
        levels.add(int(prev.size))

    for x in base_levels:
        if x <= max_n:
            levels.add(int(x))

    levels = sorted([x for x in levels if x >= 128 and x <= max_n])
    if not levels:
        levels = [512]

    # Time allocation grows superlinearly with length.
    weights = []
    for nlev in levels:
        weights.append((float(max(128, nlev)) / 512.0) ** 1.25)

    total_search = max(30.0, search_deadline - time.monotonic())
    wsum = float(sum(weights))
    scale = total_search / wsum if wsum > 0 else 0.0

    print(f"Starting search: budget={budget:.1f}s, search_deadline={max(0.0, (search_deadline - t0)):.1f}s, levels={levels}")

    for idx, nlev in enumerate(levels):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= search_deadline - 45.0:
            break

        b = max(12.0, scale * weights[idx])
        # Do not let the last levels consume the exact-validation reserve.
        b = min(b, max(12.0, search_deadline - now - 30.0))

        if b < 12.0:
            continue

        print(f"Level {idx+1}/{len(levels)}: n={nlev}, time={b:.1f}s, current_best={global_score:.8f}")
        try:
            seq, sc = run_level(nlev, b, global_seq, seed=int(seed + 1000 + idx * 131))
            if seq is not None and np.isfinite(sc):
                if sc < global_score:
                    global_score = float(sc)
                    global_seq = seq.copy()
                add_candidate_pool(candidate_pool, seq, sc)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Final long polish on the best known sequence, if time remains.
    now = time.monotonic()
    final_budget = max(0.0, search_deadline - now - 10.0)
    if final_budget > 30.0 and global_seq is not None:
        print(f"Final polish: n={int(global_seq.size)}, time={final_budget:.1f}s, best={global_score:.8f}")
        try:
            seq, sc = local_search(global_seq, final_budget, seed=int(seed + 987654321), verbose=True)
            if seq is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < global_score:
                global_score = float(sc)
                global_seq = seq.copy()
            add_candidate_pool(candidate_pool, global_seq, global_score)
        except Exception:
            pass

    add_candidate_pool(candidate_pool, global_seq, global_score)

    # Exact validation of the best fast-score candidates.
    exact_best_seq = global_seq
    exact_best_score = global_score

    if exact_best_seq is None or exact_best_seq.size == 0:
        exact_best_seq = normalize_sequence(np.ones(512, dtype=np.float64), 512.0)
        exact_best_score = fast_score_array(exact_best_seq)

    # Always exact-check the current global best.
    lst = sanitize_for_return(exact_best_seq)
    val = exact_score_list(lst)
    if np.isfinite(val):
        exact_best_score = val

    candidate_pool.sort(key=_pair_score)

    long_checked = 0
    short_checked = 0
    total_checked = 1

    for sc, seq in candidate_pool:
        now = time.monotonic()
        hard_stop = t0 + budget - 20.0
        if now >= hard_stop:
            break

        if total_checked >= 6:
            break

        l = int(seq.size) if seq is not None else 0
        if l <= 0:
            continue

        # One long exact check in addition to global; several shorter exact checks.
        if l > 8192:
            if long_checked >= 1:
                continue
            long_checked += 1
        else:
            if short_checked >= 4:
                break
            short_checked += 1

        # Skip if this is essentially the already-checked global best length and score.
        if abs(float(sc) - float(exact_best_score)) < 1e-12 and l == int(exact_best_seq.size):
            continue

        lst = sanitize_for_return(seq)
        val = exact_score_list(lst)
        total_checked += 1

        if np.isfinite(val) and val < exact_best_score:
            exact_best_score = val
            exact_best_seq = seq.copy()
        print(f"  exact check: len={l}, fast={sc:.8f}, exact={val:.8f}")

        if now >= hard_stop:
            break

    if exact_best_seq is None or exact_best_seq.size == 0:
        exact_best_seq = normalize_sequence(np.ones(512, dtype=np.float64), 512.0)

    final_list = sanitize_for_return(exact_best_seq)
    print(f"Final returned length: {len(final_list)}, best_exact={exact_best_score:.8f}")
    return final_list
```
